    FROM "Folder" AS f
    ORDER BY f."Name"
    LIMIT @__p_1 OFFSET @__p_0
) AS f1
LEFT JOIN "Folder" AS f0 ON f1."ParentFolderId" = f0."Id"
INNER JOIN "Departments" AS d ON f1."DepartmentId" = d."Id"
INNER JOIN "AspNetUsers" AS a ON f1."CreatedById" = a."Id"
LEFT JOIN "Folder" AS f2 ON f1."Id" = f2."ParentFolderId"
LEFT JOIN "Documents" AS d0 ON f1."Id" = d0."FolderId"
ORDER BY f1."Name", f1."Id", f0."Id", d."Id", a."Id", f2."Id"
